# JavaScript / TypeScript API signatures

> Generated by `node sdk/scripts/generate_api_signatures.mjs` from the current package `exports` maps using the pinned TypeScript compiler. Edit source declarations and exports, then regenerate; do not hand-edit this catalog.

Each import path lists every directly exported name and alias, linking to one shared signature definition per underlying source declaration. It includes visible function and type parameters, defaults, class/interface properties, getters/setters, and methods; underscore and ECMAScript private names are omitted. Runtime behavior, errors, permissions, platform support, and interaction guarantees remain documented in [JS_API.md](JS_API.md) and the specialized references linked there.

Source-first reuse note: outside the retained parent `pocket_host/`, this work checked `README.md`, `docs/02_ARCHITECTURE_AND_MEMORY_DESIGN.md`, `docs/03_DUAL_HOST_UI_COMPONENTS_SPEC.md`, `docs/04_DEVELOPER_GUIDE_AND_VERIFICATION.md`, and `docs/04_BIDIRECTIONAL_FFI_WASM_AND_YOOASSET_HOTUPDATE.md` in `/Volumes/ORICO/Development/Github/pocketjs_flutter_demo`. These preserve the earlier Flutter/WeChat host split, component-to-host projection notes, and run/regression scenarios. Their historical 38-component and QuickJS/WeChat-specific claims are not treated as this SDK API; the necessary adaptation is to enumerate current package exports, with UI policy and composition owned by JS. Validation uses TypeScript parser/checker exports, default-value examples, and source SHA markers.

Coverage: 3565 importable export names, 1898 shared signature definitions, and 3620 listed members across 117 direct import paths. Source digests cover 176 inventoried files.

<a id="api-sig-df7202e1fe1b2e9c"></a>
### `StoryInstruction`

**Type** ``StoryInstruction = | { readonly type: "say"; /** Stable and unique within this story; used by read/skip metadata. */ readonly id: string; /** Optional visual layout for this line; omitted keeps the legacy dialogue panel. */ readonly presentation?: NovelSayPresentation; readonly speaker?: string; readonly portrait?: string; /** Audio registry cue played for this line; auto mode waits for its real completion. */ readonly voice?: string; /** Supports literal text and `{{variableName}}` interpolation. */ readonly text: string; /** Optional stable inline annotation ranges measured against this canonical text. */ readonly rich?: NovelSayRich; } | { readonly type: "scene"; /** Omitted keeps the current background; null clears it. */ readonly background?: string | null; /** Omitted preserves the current overlay planes; [] removes them. Array order is back-to-front. */ readonly backgroundLayers?: readonly NovelBackgroundLayer[]; readonly characters?: readonly SceneCharacterUpdate[]; readonly remove?: readonly string[]; /** Omitted preserves the current objects; [] clears them; otherwise replaces the object set. */ readonly objects?: readonly NovelSceneObject[]; readonly transition?: SceneTransition; /** Omitted preserves the current camera. Without cameraMotion, setting it is immediate. */ readonly camera?: NovelCameraTransform; /** Camera movement can run independently of the scene fade/dissolve. Requires camera. */ readonly cameraMotion?: NovelCameraMotion; /** Starts a reusable timeline preset; omitted or null interrupts the current one. */ readonly timeline?: string | null; } | { readonly type: "audio"; readonly cue: string; readonly action?: "play" | "stop"; readonly channel?: string; readonly loop?: boolean; readonly group?: NovelAuthoredAudioGroup; } | { readonly type: "wait"; readonly durationMs: number } | NovelVariableInstruction | { readonly type: "branch"; readonly when: Condition; readonly then: string; readonly else: string; } | { readonly type: "jump"; readonly target: string } | { readonly type: "call"; readonly target: string } | { readonly type: "return" } | { readonly type: "choice"; readonly id: string; readonly prompt?: string; readonly options: readonly ChoiceOptionDefinition[]; readonly timeLimitMs?: number; /** Label selected when the timer expires. */ readonly timeoutTarget?: string; } | NovelInputInstruction | NovelTradeInstruction | NovelVideoInstruction | { readonly type: "ending"; readonly id: string; readonly text?: string; readonly cg?: string }``
